Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the homological object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 852. The moduli space has dimension at most 944 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_852(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 852. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^944(S, F) is trivial. QED.
